Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple ***Magic** 2011* draft from the Top 8 of a Premier Event on **Magic Online**. You can find a full Premier Event schedule [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20140412005841/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/Digital/MagicOnline.aspx?x=mtg/digital/magiconline/calendar).

Enjoy!

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da558_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20101013000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=1657172)
